Errors that occur early in execution may propagate through both the agent context and environment state, and their effects may be difficult to reverse through subsequent actions. Existing methods mainly seek to reduce such errors through plan refinement and safety checks but provide little support after errors occur. To enable recovery during long-horizon execution, we present AgentRewind, a runtime recovery framework that records aligned checkpoints of the agent context and controlled environment, allowing agents to return to an earlier state and resume execution with information from previous attempts. We also construct MettleBench, a benchmark for evaluating task completion and partial progress on long-horizon engineering assignments containing a series of related requirements. Experiments across tasks, multiple models, execution strategies, and agent harnesses show that AgentRewind improves task success rate and average checklist progress over the compared baselines.

</details>


### [170] [Tripwire: Triggering Aligned Refusal via Statistically Certified Safety Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14392)
*Wei Zhao,Zhe Li,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tripwire，一种无需训练的大语言模型越狱防御方法。通过FDR控制的逐神经元假设检验和效用特异性过滤器识别安全专属神经元，然后采用触发式钳位（trigger-style clamp）将这些神经元固定在有害条件均值激活上，形成内部有害输入信号以触发对齐期间学到的拒答行为。该方法可通过检测器门控推理时干预或离线偏置补丁权重编辑两种等效部署实现。在四个安全对齐LLM和四种攻击下，平均攻击成功率降至2.0%以下，同时MT-Bench效用损失仅0.5%-5.3%，为所有防御方法中最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经元/路径级别的LLM越狱防御存在两大不足：一是抑制有毒神经元会因有害语义在网络中分布广泛而需要大范围干预，严重损害模型效用；二是基于外部分类器识别安全神经元的方法也会误伤对效用重要的神经元。此外，现有方法均为始终开启模式，即使没有攻击也会干扰正常良性请求。因此需要一种既能精准定位安全专属神经元、又能按需触发的非侵入式防御方法。

Method: Tripwire方法包含三步：1) 通过逐神经元假设检验（per-neuron hypothesis test）在FDR（错误发现率）控制下识别安全特异性神经元，并辅以效用特异性过滤器排除对模型性能重要的神经元；2) 对选定的神经元施加触发式钳位（trigger-style clamp），将其固定在有害条件均值激活值上，从而注入内部有害输入信号，触发模型在安全对齐阶段学到的拒答行为；3) 提供两种数学上等价的部署模式：检测器门控的推理时干预（detector-gated inference-time intervention）和离线偏置补丁权重编辑（offline bias-patch weight edit）。

Result: 在四个安全对齐LLM（包括不同规模和架构的模型）和四种代表性越狱攻击的广泛实验中，Tripwire将平均攻击成功率（ASR）降至至多2.0%，同时MT-Bench上的效用损失仅为0.5%至5.3%，是所有对比防御方法中效用损失最小的。证明了该方法在保持防御有效性的同时，显著降低了对模型正常能力的损害。

Conclusion: Tripwire通过精准识别安全专属神经元并采用触发式钳位机制，实现了训练自由的、按需启用的越狱防御，在极低攻击成功率的同时几乎不影响模型效用。两种等价的部署模式（推理时干预和离线权重编辑）提供了灵活的应用选择，为LLM安全防御提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Neuron- and path-level interventions offer the finest-grained route to defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, yet existing methods fall short of this promise, i.e., they often compromise model utility significantly. Specifically, one line of work suppresses toxic neurons to erase harmful semantics, but since such semantics are distributed across the network, blocking every pathway forces a large intervention footprint. An alternative line of research focus on identify safety neurons using external classifiers. While promising, the existing approaches suffer from compromising neurons that are important for the model utility as well. Moreover, both approaches remain always on and thus perturb every benign request even when no attack is present. To address these limitations, we present \ours{}, a training-free defense that first identifies safety-specific neurons through per-neuron hypothesis tests under false-discovery-rate control together with a utility-specificity filter. Based on this identification, a trigger-style clamp holds the selected neurons at their harmful-conditional mean activations, injecting an internal harmful-input signal that triggers the refusal behavior learned during alignment. The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [171] [LLMs Don't Pay for the Jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14397)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证大语言模型无法进行真正的溯因推理（abduction），原因并非缺乏具身模拟，而是缺乏"热力学耦合"——即认知错误与物理成本之间的关联机制。作者以普朗克1900年提出E=hν为例，说明该假设既非归纳也非演绎所能产生，其采纳依赖于对知识错误与物理代价的耦合，而固定权重的transformer推理无论规模大小都缺乏这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将大模型溯因推理的局限归因于缺乏具身模拟，但学界对此存疑。作者希望找到一个更根本的解释：揭示真正阻碍机器溯因的因素可能不在于身体/感知经验，而在于系统内部是否存在认知错误与物理成本的耦合机制。

Method: 采用思想实验与理论论证结合的方式：以普朗克黑体辐射问题（E=hν）作为溯因推理的典型案例，论证该假设无法由归纳或演绎推出；引入"热力学耦合"概念来形式化认知错误与物理成本之间的关系；并通过实验数据（输出熵在不同因果难度任务中几乎不变，而准确率从100%降至17%）佐证固定权重transformer缺乏此种耦合。

Result: 实证结果表明，随着因果难度急剧增加（准确率从100%降至17%），transformer的输出熵几乎保持不变，说明模型内部不存在使认知错误产生物理代价进而强制修正的机制。

Conclusion: 机器溯因推理缺失的关键可能比具身模拟更深层：系统必须具有某种物理机制，使认知错误变得足够昂贵以强制触发理论修订。这一发现为可解释的推理架构设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Zahavy [2026] argues that Large Language Models, despite their capabilities in induction and deduction, cannot perform the abductive "Jump" that produced Einstein's equivalence principle, and attributes this limitation to the absence of embodied simulation. Zheng-Xin [2026] and Farmer [2026] question whether embodiment is necessary for abduction, pointing to alternative routes to General Relativity and forms of abduction that require no sensorimotor grounding. Max Planck resolved the blackbody radiation problem in 1900. Planck's move to E = hν required no embodied simulation. It was motivated by a mathematical consequence of classical theory, an infinite predicted energy for a finite measured quantity, that could not be physically accepted. We show that neither induction nor deduction could have produced the postulate and argue that its adoption required a coupling between epistemic error and physical cost. We formalize this distinction through thermodynamic coupling and show that fixed-weight transformer inference lacks such coupling, regardless of model scale. This is consistent with empirical results showing that output entropy remains nearly unchanged across tasks with sharply increasing causal difficulty, even as accuracy falls from 100% to 17%. We therefore argue that the missing ingredient in machine abduction may lie deeper than embodiment: a system must have some physical mechanism through which epistemic error becomes costly enough to force revision.

</details>


### [172] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能科学家（AI Scientists）的过去与未来——即能够自动化科学研究的机器。以Adam和Eve为早期代表，AI科学家正从单个组件自动化走向系统集成，有望使科学更快、更便宜、更系统化且更具可重复性，并为Nobel Turing Challenge（2050年前实现诺贝尔级别自动发现）设立路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学自动化不再受限于单个组件的可行性，核心瓶颈在于如何将神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、仿真、实验设计、机器人和正式科学记录等要素整合为统一、通用的科学智能体，从而变革科学实践。

Method: 采用综述型方法，梳理AI科学家的历史演进（从Adam到Eve再到基于基础模型和自主智能体的现代系统），分析其架构范式（自我驱动实验室、自主实验循环、多智能体协作），并提出以"集成"为核心的研究框架。

Result: 指出自动化科学的关键问题已从"能否自动化单个组件"转变为"如何集成各组件"；Adam和Eve已证明物理实验+假说形成循环的可行性，基础模型与机器人技术的进步使更通用的AI科学家成为现实，Nobel Turing Challenge进展超前于计划。

Conclusion: AI科学家有望创造新的科学形态并改变世界：不仅能加速和降低科学成本，还能探索人类难以单独处理的复杂系统，并支持数千个AI科学家协同解决单一问题；到2050年实现诺贝尔级自动发现是现实可行且正在加速推进的目标。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [173] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出optstop，一种基于精度感知的自适应停止框架，将LLM评估视为序贯测量问题：在不确定性高的地方继续采样，在估计精确或稳定处停止。通过分层贝叶斯推断支撑二值、有序和连续结果，可在线或回溯运行，在200项10轮评估中减少了57%-97%的计划试验，且结论与完整运行等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LLM评估使用固定采样预算，对每个测试项分配相同的采样次数，即使某些项的估计已经足够精确仍继续采样，造成计算资源浪费。作者希望将评估计算按不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配。

Method: 提出optstop框架，将评估建模为序贯测量问题。构建在分层贝叶斯推断之上，支持二值、有序和连续结果；每个benchmark项都保持采样资格，无需校准的题库；可在线运行或事后回溯运行；包含一个安全机制：当测得的性能趋近于零时（罕见成功至关重要），采样会更谨慎。

Result: 在200项、10轮的示例评估中，在九个验证设置下移除了57%-97%的计划试验，且整体结论与完整运行等效。

Conclusion: LLM评估的计算资源可以依据不确定性而非固定重复次数来分配，节省幅度取决于评估设计的具体情况。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [174] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI辅助AI研发可能引发的智能爆炸的数学基础，发现奇点式增长（垂直渐近线）比经济学启发的建模所预测的更难以实现，并指出一类被忽视的「快于指数但不趋于无穷」的增长速率，同时强调生成时间（反馈回路周期）是决定智能爆炸行为的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI被用于加速AI研发，智能爆炸的反馈回路可能产生能力急剧升级。作者希望从数学上理解这一最剧烈可能性的动态机制，澄清现有经济学启发模型的不足，并识别真正驱动智能爆炸行为的核心参数。

Method: 通过数学建模分析智能爆炸反馈回路的增长动力学，审视极端（最具爆炸性）情形下的增长行为，系统比较指数增长、超指数增长以及奇点增长（垂直渐近线）的边界条件，重点考察生成时间这一被忽视的参数对系统行为的影响。

Result: 奇点式增长（趋向垂直渐近线）比近期经济学启发建模所预期的更难实现；存在一类被忽视的重要增长速率——快于指数但不会导致垂直渐近线；生成时间（反馈回路循环所需时间）是关键变量，奇点增长要求生成时间迅速趋近于零。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸的奇点模型比以往认为的更严苛，生成时间而非其他因素在决定是否存在垂直渐近线方面起决定性作用；这提示对AI爆炸性发展的预测需要重新审视其数学模型假设。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [175] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE-Bench,一个基于物理仿真器的自进化智能体基准测试,包含144个源到目标环境适配对,横跨六个物理领域。该基准测试了智能体在环境条件改变后的恢复能力,发现现有方法远未饱和,且仿真器引导的反思方法比未经验证的自我修订更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数自进化智能体评估在固定执行条件下进行,未测试环境条件改变后的恢复能力。实际应用中环境经常变化,需要专门的基准来评估智能体在此类情境下的自适应能力。

Method: 构建包含六个物理领域、144个源到目标适配对的仿真器基准。每个适配对将源环境的代码驱动设计映射到具有相同目标和界面的突变目标环境,要求智能体在有限尝试预算内,利用诊断性沙盒反馈,迭代改进代码直到在目标环境中成功工作。比较了来自四个范式的十种自进化方法。

Result: 基准远未饱和:Reflexion+Qwen3-14B仅在完整基准的35.9%适配对上成功,GPT-5.5在全预算下仅解决Statics子集的66.7%。仿真器引导的反思优于未验证的自我修订;记忆锚定会限制探索;宽树搜索会探索但无法收敛。即使揭示确切的物理变化,性能上限也未被提高。

Conclusion: 测试结果表明,仿真器引导的反思是更可靠的自进化策略,而机制重新设计而非参数推断是当前方法的核心瓶颈。PACE-Bench为未来自进化智能体研究提供了重要评估基准。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [176] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wyvern是一个多智能体框架，用于自动生成有依据的多模态技术报告，通过引入声明自动修订阶段确保内容接地（grounding），实验表明其在图表信息量、报告有用性和引用召回率/精确率方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动的创新时代，知识增长速度极快，人类难以跟上。虽生成模型被广泛用于合成内容，但往往缺乏信息依据（grounding），需要一种能够生成有依据、多模态技术报告的自动化框架。

Method: 提出了Wyvern多智能体框架，支持生成融合图像、表格、文本及支撑引用的统一多模态报告；特别设计了声明自动修订（claims auto-revision）阶段以确保内容的接地性。采用人工评估和自动评估两种方式验证框架质量。

Result: 人工评估显示：Wyvern生成的图的informativeness在87%的案例中被认为优于近期基线；Wyvern的报告在63%至100%的实例中被评为比三种替代方法更有用。自动评估显示：与基线相比，Wyvern在引用召回率上提升最高达2.3倍，引用精确率上提升达1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern框架通过多智能体协作和声明自动修订机制，能够有效生成既有依据又具有多模态形式的技术报告，在引用质量和报告实用性上显著超越现有基线方法。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [177] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SheetCompass，一个基于图结构和记忆驱动的智能体框架，用于电子表格推理与自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将电子表格展平为序列字符串，丢失了表内边界和表间语义，导致LLM无法利用人类专家在检查电子表格时自然使用的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass显式建模工作表内和工作表间的结构关系，同时在记忆中维护任务相关信息，使智能体能够在复杂工作簿上进行更有效的推理。

Result: （摘要中未提供具体实验数据，仅给出框架设计。）

Conclusion: 通过图引导和记忆驱动的机制增强LLM对电子表格的推理能力，弥补传统展平方法丢失结构信息的缺陷。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [178] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种移位感知双编码器迁移学习框架用于PM2.5短期预测，在源域（美国10个监测站）预处理编码器，并在目标域（台湾77个站点）进行适应和评估。冻结源编码器模型在主要基线中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960, R²=0.8725），而允许源编码器适应取得了最优整体结果（MSE=21.6575, R²=0.8739）。消融实验表明台湾特化分支最为关键，SHAP分析显示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测和气象变量驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时程PM2.5预测在目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异时仍然困难：仅用本地数据训练的模型可能无法捕捉复杂的时间动态，而直接迁移学习可能导致负迁移。因此需要一种既能结合源域知识又能保持目标域特定表征的框架。

Method: 构建移位感知双编码器迁移框架，包含源编码器（在美国10个监测站的逐时观测上预训练）和目标域专属分支。在台湾77个站点的两年逐时观测数据上，采用时间顺序的训练-验证-测试协议进行适应与评估，并与四个主要基线比较。通过消融分析验证各组件贡献，利用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四个基线中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960, MAE=3.1597, R²=0.8725），较TL-v1和TL-v2分别降低约7.1%和4.1%的MSE。消融实验显示移除台湾特化分支导致性能下降最大。允许源编码器适应得到最优结果（MSE=21.6575, MAE=3.1383, R²=0.8739）。SHAP表明近期PM2.5观测和与污染物输运/扩散相关的气象变量为主要预测驱动因素。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特定信息且使迁移表征在目标监督下进行适应时最为有效。该框架有效缓解了负迁移问题，为跨区域PM2.5短期预测提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [179] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测试时世界模型推理（Twin）系统，让前沿编码智能体为连续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）编写可执行的世界模型。该系统在不了解每局游戏规则和目标的前提下，仅通过模拟与交互来构建世界模型。Twin在183个关卡中清除了179个（97.8%），其中158个（88.3%）比人类更高效。在目标推断方面，87.2%的关卡在获得任何奖励前就推断出目标。相比基础模型直接游玩的7.8%得分和现成harness的61.1%，Twin将同一基础模型的得分提升至93.3%。研究表明构建可用的世界模型比预想中简单，而更困难的问题在于正确推断目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个任务手工设计世界模型（每个游戏一个定制设计），这种方法不可扩展且耗费人力。作者希望探索是否可以让前沿编码智能体通过模拟和交互自动编写可执行的世界模型，从而解决连续学习任务中的泛化和适应性问题。

Method: Twin系统采用测试时世界模型推理方法。核心流程包括：(1) 编码智能体根据观察到的游戏交互编写可执行世界模型；(2) 回放验证在孪生世界模型中进行，系统强制要求只有在程序能够复现所有先前观察到的游戏转换后才允许执行动作；(3) 每次世界模型预测与实际动作结果不匹配都会产生一个反例，用于修复世界模型。系统在游戏过程中持续通过模拟和交互完善模型，并使用搜索来发现未知目标。

Result: Twin清除了183个关卡中的179个（97.8%），在179个清除关卡中158个（88.3%）比人类首次游玩更高效。在156个清除关卡（87.2%）中，系统在获得任何奖励前就推断出了目标，其余关卡通过搜索自动发现目标。在0-100的基准评分中，基础模型直接游玩仅得7.8%，现成harness提升至61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型提升至93.3%，清除了25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 作者发现构建可用的世界模型比预期简单，而真正的挑战在于目标推断。Twin的成功表明，将代码生成能力与测试时世界模型推理相结合，可以大幅提升智能体在连续学习任务上的表现，同时揭示了世界模型构建与目标推断之间的不对称难度。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [180] [Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509)
*Zhelun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"读取证据"与"综合证据"分离的双阶段框架，通过四字段证据元组统一两阶段设计，并揭示了"计数尺度漂移"这一组合系统的失效模式，主张用校准的对数似然比池化代替阈值投票，并在纵向语料库上（结果揭晓前后两个场景）验证了该原则，后者达到0.921 AUPRC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将多个来源的证据拼接进单个提示词让语言模型直接得出结论，这混淆了两种需求不同的操作：解读单个来源需要容量和上下文，而综合多个解读需要固定算术、跨实例可比性以及"可以什么都不返回"的能力。作者希望分离这两个过程并解决它们之间的接口设计问题，同时指出该分离揭示了一个此前未被明确识别的失效模式。

Method: 提出四字段证据元组（假设、可靠性桶、理由、溯源）作为两阶段的固定接口。通过理论分析证明：对未归一化权重求和做阈值化恰好等价于后验阈值化，但工作点随来源数量漂移（计数尺度漂移），且漂移幅度随读者可靠性增长；当来源可靠性不同时，投票规则与后验对实例的排序不一致，无法用任何阈值调和。解决方案是池化已校准的对数似然比，这是一种纯算术修复，适用于评分求和式分诊引擎、阳性计数诊断面板、叠加多信号检测器等多类规则。

Result: 在纵向语料库上两次实例化该原则：结果揭晓前场景中，使用小型序列编码器（简易辅助目标）加树集成（承载删失生存损失）达到0.921 AUPRC，优于手工基线的0.805。论文还区分了可迁移与需按领域重估的组件，并提出五个可证伪预测、三个阴性结果以及仍受混淆的比较。

Conclusion: 分离"解读"与"综合"、用校准似然比池化替代统一提示词，能够同时修复计数尺度漂移和不可调和排序问题，且该修复是算术性的而非架构性的；同一分离原则在结果揭晓前后两个阶段都有效，只是粒度不同——前者体现在阅读层面，后者体现在学习能力层面。

Abstract: Systems that ask a language model to reach a conclusion from many sources usually concatenate them into one prompt. This conflates two operations with different requirements. Interpreting a source rewards capacity and context. Combining interpretations rewards fixed arithmetic, comparability across instances, and the option to return nothing. Once separated, the design problem becomes the interface between them. We propose a four-field evidence tuple (hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance) and show that fixing it determines both halves. The separation also reveals a failure mode in how such systems combine, which we call count-scale drift. Thresholding a sum of unnormalized weights is exactly posterior thresholding, but at an operating point that slides with the number of sources consulted. The slide grows with reader reliability. When source reliabilities differ, the vote rule and the posterior order instances differently, and no threshold reconciles them. Pooling calibrated log-likelihood ratios addresses both problems. The fix is arithmetic rather than architectural, and applies to a class of rules beyond language models: score-summing triage engines, diagnostic panels scored by counting positives, and additive multi-signal detectors. We then instantiate the principle twice on one longitudinal corpus, once after outcomes resolve and once before. The same partition helps in both, at different granularities: over reading in the first, over learning capacity in the second. There, a small sequence encoder on an easy auxiliary objective plus a tree ensemble carrying the censored survival loss reaches 0.921 AUPRC against 0.805 for a hand-crafted baseline. We separate what transfers from what must be re-estimated per domain, and state five predictions that would falsify the framework, three negative results, and which comparisons remain confounded.

</details>


### [181] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究道德AI偏好引导管道中三个关键决策——特征范围界定、选民采样和问题框架——如何影响聚合偏好结果。基于809名参与者、三个部署场景（肾脏分配、模拟缺席员工、AI生成死者形象）的两阶段实验，发现道德相关特征因场景而异、政治意识形态影响约三分之一特征的偏好方向、问题措辞可将意识形态差距扩大或缩小至一个量级。作者认为投票式对齐无法仅靠聚合实现公平透明AI，管道各阶段应被审计和披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在越来越多的道德敏感决策中发挥作用，道德偏好引导成为主流回应方式。然而在投票之前，开发者做出的三个关键选择（特征范围、选民采样、问题框架）往往被视为技术细节而非规范性选择，其影响未被系统审查。本文旨在揭示这些看似技术性的选择如何在经验上塑造道德AI引导所产生的偏好。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计（N=809），在三个部署场景（AI肾脏分配、模拟缺席员工的AI代理、AI生成逝者形象）中系统比较三个管道阶段。第一阶段考察道德相关特征是否跨场景转移；第二阶段检验政治意识形态对特征偏好的影响；第三阶段操纵引导问题的措辞，测量其对意识形态差距及道德基础关联的影响。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）道德相关特征跨场景存在显著差异，特征图式不能假定跨部署领域转移；（2）约三分之一的特征偏好随政治意识形态差异而变化，部分差异方向相反，选民池的意识形态构成会影响聚合偏好；（3）问题措辞可将意识形态差距扩大或收窄达一个完整量表点，且框架条件改变道德基础与判断之间的关联。

Conclusion: 投票式对齐无法仅通过聚合本身实现公平或透明的AI。道德AI引导管道的每个阶段都应被审计和公开披露，因为它们各自都会实质性影响最终产生的偏好和道德判断。这些发现对当前以道德偏好引导为基础的AI对齐实践提出了方法论和治理层面的挑战。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [182] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型应用中"会话交接"（session handover）的方法论与理论性质。将交接形式化为任务相关上下文学习（ICL）状态的迁移，区分了早期材料的精确恢复与目标分布的保持。在外部性条件下，预测等价性刻画了最粗的确定性充分交接，并给出固定长度的比特需求。提出了三部分记录方案，并在高斯线性回归和非参数回归中给出了定量的记忆-误差边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型应用中，当上下文达到模型输入限制、应用重启或任务移交给其他代理时，任务需要在新会话中继续。此时必须决定从先前会话传递哪些信息，但现有的交接策略缺乏系统的理论框架来指导这一决策——即"哪些信息必须保留、保留多少才能保证任务延续的可靠性"。

Method: 1) 将交接形式化为任务相对ICL状态的迁移；2) 区分精确恢复与分布保持两种目标；3) 在外部性条件下，利用预测等价性刻画最粗确定性充分交接，导出固定长度比特需求；4) 隔离记忆约束、书写者与延续过程各自的效应，量化在得知下游查询前书写的代价；5) 提出三部分记录——精确存储决策与约束、使用任务合理统计量压缩重复证据、保留统计量无法保留效应的原始观测；6) 在高斯线性回归与非参数回归中分别给出精确有限维交接和有限比特扰动界以及记忆-均方预测误差的上下界。

Result: 高斯线性回归场景下可得到精确的有限维交接表示和有限比特扰动界；非参数回归场景下得到记忆量与平方预测误差之间的上下界关系，表明记忆需求取决于延续任务的性质。

Conclusion: 本文为会话交接提供了系统的理论与方法：明确了交接必须保留什么信息（由延续任务决定），以及记忆需求如何随延续任务而变化，为大语言模型应用中的会话持久化提供了理论依据和操作指南。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Secret-Stego Dissimilarity as a Design Axis: Invertible Coverless Image Steganography with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13597)
*Hongxin Xu,Jianping Mei,Can Wang,Defang Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出InvCISD，一种基于可逆扩散框架的无载体图像隐写方法，通过LIMNet可逆网络耦合秘密图像与无关参考图像的潜在表示，在保持重建质量的同时大幅降低秘密图像与隐写图像之间的视觉相似性，提升隐写安全性与图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的CIS方法虽然能生成自然的隐写图像，但隐写图像与秘密图像之间存在显著视觉相似性，容易暴露结构和语义线索，带来安全隐患。仅用恢复保真度评估不够，需要在不牺牲隐写质量和恢复保真度的情况下实现秘密与隐写图像之间的视觉不相似性。

Method: 提出InvCISD框架，包含两阶段训练：首先在扩散潜在空间中训练LIMNet可逆网络以耦合秘密图像与无关参考图像的潜在表示，然后对整个网络（LIMNet与扩散反演及生成模块集成的整体）进行端到端微调。

Result: 实验表明该方法大幅降低了秘密-隐写图像的视觉相似性，提升了隐写图像质量，同时保持满意的秘密图像重建质量。进一步研究发现所有被评估方法均易被面向CIS的隐写分析模型检测到。

Conclusion: InvCISD在视觉不相似性、隐写质量和重建保真度之间取得了良好平衡，但对抗定向隐写分析的鲁棒性仍是未来CIS研究的關鍵方向。

Abstract: Coverless image steganography (CIS) synthesizes a stego image rather than modifying an existing cover image, enabling authorized recipients to reconstruct the original secret image from the stego. Existing diffusion-based CIS methods can generate natural-looking stego images but preserve substantial visual similarity to the secret image. This resemblance risks exposing structural and semantic cues, giving rise to security vulnerabilities that cannot be evaluated solely via recovery fidelity. Achieving substantial visual dissimilarity between the secret and stego images without compromising stego quality and recovery fidelity remains challenging. To address this issue, we propose InvCISD, an invertible diffusion framework that couples the latent representations of the secret and an irrelevant reference image with an invertible network called LIMNet. We first train LIMNet in diffusion latent space, followed by end-to-end fine-tuning of the entire network, i.e., LIMNet integrated diffusion inversion and generation modules. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method substantially reduces secret-stego visual similarity, improves stego quality, and retains satisfactory secret reconstruction quality. Our further investigation shows that all evaluated methods are highly detectable by the CIS-oriented steganalysis model, indicating that resistance against targeted steganalysis constitutes a critical direction for future CIS research.

</details>


### [184] [LoopVSR: A Loop Engineering Framework for Automated Repair of Visual Speech Recognition Inference Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13610)
*Fei Qin,Bowen Zhang,Chao Fan,Pengcheng Luo,Genke Yang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: LoopVSR是一个基于循环工程（Loop Engineering）的框架，利用代码代理通过端到端执行证据自动诊断和修复视觉语音识别（VSR）推理流水线。它将仓库级诊断与补丁修复同外部控制器相结合，通过真实推理、字符错误率（CER）等反馈信号构建闭环，逐步暴露被上游故障掩盖的下游缺陷。在CMLR VSR系统上，LoopVSR修复了全部11个主要故障（100%平均恢复率），而静态守卫仅修复2/11（18.13%恢复率），并在独立隐藏测试集上保持了修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VSR推理流水线包括视频解码、嘴部区域提取、预处理、模型调用和译码等多个阶段，其多阶段特性导致上游失败会掩盖下游故障。当前流水线维护主要依赖预定义检查和人工调试，缺乏自动化诊断和修复机制。

Method: LoopVSR采用循环工程框架，让代码代理利用每次真实推理产生的端到端执行证据（新异常、张量统计、识别错误）自动诊断并修复推理流水线。框架包含两个核心部分：一是约束性的仓库级诊断与补丁修复模块，二是外部控制器负责审查修改、运行真实推理并通过失败信号和CER决定接受或回滚补丁。这种反馈循环逐步暴露被上游故障掩盖的问题。

Result: 在CMLR VSR系统上，LoopVSR修复了全部11个主要故障，达到100%平均恢复率；对比方法静态守卫仅修复2/11个故障，平均恢复率18.13%。此外LoopVSR在7次接受的迭代中解决了三个级联任务，并在独立的200视频隐藏测试集上保持了修复效果。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明LoopVSR实现了VSR推理流水线的可量化、端到端自动化修复，显著优于静态守卫方法，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual speech recognition (VSR) recovers speech from lip movements when audio is noisy or unavailable. Its multi-stage inference pipeline spans video decoding, mouth-region extraction, preprocessing, model invocation, and decoding, where upstream failures can mask downstream faults. Pipeline maintenance therefore still relies largely on predefined checks and manual debugging. We propose LoopVSR, a Loop Engineering framework that enables a code agent to automatically diagnose and repair VSR inference pipelines using end-to-end execution evidence. It couples constrained repository-level diagnosis and patching with an external controller that audits changes, runs real inference, and accepts or rolls back patches using failures and character error rate (CER). The resulting feedback loop returns newly observed exceptions, tensor statistics, and recognition errors to the agent, progressively exposing faults masked by upstream failures. On the CMLR VSR system, LoopVSR repairs all 11 main faults with 100% mean recovery, whereas the Static guard repairs 2 of 11 with 18.13% mean recovery. It also resolves three cascading tasks in seven accepted iterations and preserves recovery on an independent 200-video hidden set. These results demonstrate that LoopVSR enables measurable, end-to-end automated repair of VSR inference pipelines.

</details>


### [185] [TRUE-Colon: Exposing a Consistent Transfer Asymmetry in Real-Time Polyp Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13711)
*Sebastian Doerrich,Andreas Franz Schwab,Francesco Di Salvo,Shyam Nandan Rai,Hanh Huyen My Nguyen,Christian Ledig*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUE-Colon标准化基准协议，通过在60段未剪辑完整肠镜视频上评估四种实时架构，揭示了CADe系统在精选病灶基准与真实临床部署之间的迁移不对称性：仅用精选片段训练的模型在完整流程上性能严重下降，而用完整流程训练的模型在真实数据上表现更优且保持精选基准的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CADe系统在真实临床部署中可靠性不足，根源在于模型开发依赖仅覆盖病灶的精选数据集，无法代表日常检查中常见的冗长阴性片段和操作伪影。这种结构缺陷导致在病灶中心基准上评估产生"成功假象"。

Method: 建立TRUE-Colon标准化基准协议，同时测量定位精度与部署关键特性；在SUN和PICCOLO精选基准及60段未编辑的完整肠镜视频（REAL-Colon）上，对比评估四种实时架构：Faster R-CNN、YOLOv8、YOLOv11和RT-DETR。

Result: 发现一致的迁移不对称性：仅用精选片段训练的模型在完整流程中性能严重崩溃；而用完整流程训练的模型在REAL-Colon上能显著提高对非息肉内容的拒绝率，且基本保持精选基准的准确率。Transformer检测器（RT-DETR）敏感性最强且检测最早最持久，卷积检测器在更高吞吐量下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 可部署CADe的训练与基准评估应从精选病灶中心片段转向完整流程数据和部署相关工作点，以弥合临床转化差距。

Abstract: Computer-aided detection (CADe) systems for colonoscopy promise to reduce clinical miss rates, yet reliable real-world deployment remains elusive. This translational gap stems in part from a structural flaw in model development: the reliance on curated datasets that under-represent the long negative stretches and procedure-related artifacts characteristic of routine examinations. Training and evaluating architectures strictly on these lesion-centric benchmarks creates an illusion of success, since such benchmarks cannot capture clinically crucial metrics. To expose this gap, we establish TRUE-Colon, a standardized benchmarking protocol that measures key deployment characteristics alongside localization accuracy, and evaluate four real-time architectures (Faster R-CNN, YOLOv8, YOLOv11, RT-DETR) across curated benchmarks (SUN, PICCOLO) and 60 unedited, full-length procedures (REAL-Colon). We observe a consistent transfer asymmetry: models trained strictly on curated clips suffer a severe performance collapse when evaluated on full procedures, whereas procedure-trained models substantially improve rejection of non-polyp content on REAL-Colon, and largely retain their accuracy on curated benchmarks. Beyond transferability, we find that the Transformer detector attains the strongest sensitivity and the earliest, most persistent detections, while the convolutional detectors stay competitive at a higher throughput. Together, these results indicate that both training and benchmarking for deployable CADe should shift from curated, lesion-centric clips toward full-procedure data and deployment-relevant operating points. Source code is available at https://github.com/sdoerrich97/true-colon.

</details>


### [186] [VLM- and LLM-Driven Multi-Agent System for PET Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13791)
*Boxiao Yu,Savas Ozdemir,Yang Xing,Fumio Hashimoto,Jiong Wu,Yizhou Chen,Axel Rominger,Ruogu Fang,Kuangyu Shi,Tinsu Pan,Kuang Gong*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）和大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体PET去噪框架，该框架能够动态评估图像质量和病灶状态，自主选择最优去噪模型和参数，并通过闭环反馈和回滚机制实现自适应PET图像增强。在Siemens Biograph Vision Quadra PET/CT数据（1/20和1/50低剂量设置）上的实验表明，该框架在PSNR和SSIM指标上优于UNet、GAN和DDPM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET成像受限于空间分辨率和信噪比，尽管深度学习去噪方法表现优异，但其实际部署面临挑战——通常需要多个专用模型和专家干预（如识别运动伪影、估计噪声水平以选择去噪器、去噪后病灶定量评估）。VLM和LLM的进展为实现自动化、决策驱动的工作流提供了新机遇。

Method: 提出一个由VLM和LLM驱动的多智能体PET去噪框架：1）VLM评估图像质量和病灶状态；2）LLM进行上下文推理，动态决策选择最优去噪模型和参数；3）包含闭环反馈和回滚机制，实现自适应策略调整。模块化设计支持对各智能体组件进行独立评估，并在多剂量水平下与UNet、GAN和DDPM基线进行对比。

Result: 个体模块评估证明了智能体组件的可靠性；完整框架在1/20和1/50两个低剂量水平下均获得了比UNet、GAN和DDPM基线更高的PSNR和SSIM值。

Conclusion: 初步结果证明了使用闭环多智能体框架根据不同图像条件自适应调整PET去噪策略的可行性，为PET图像质量增强的自动化、智能化工作流提供了新路径。

Abstract: Positron emission tomography (PET) imaging suffers from limited spatial resolution and low signal-to-noise ratio, which can compromise quantitative accuracy and lesion detectability. Deep learning-based denoising methods have demonstrated strong potential for improving PET image quality. However, their practical deployment in real-world settings remains challenging, often requiring multiple specialized models and expert interventions, such as identifying motion-induced misregistration artifacts, estimating noise levels to select an appropriate denoiser, and performing lesion-focused quantitative assessment after denoising. Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) for image quality understanding and large language models (LLMs) for contextual reasoning provide new opportunities for automated, decision-driven workflows. Inspired by expert workflows for PET image quality enhancement, we propose an VLM- and LLM-driven multi-agent PET denoising framework that dynamically assesses image quality and lesion status, autonomously selects optimal denoising models and parameters, and enables closed-loop feedback with rollback mechanisms. Experiments were conducted on Siemens Biograph Vision Quadra PET/CT data with 1/20 and 1/50 low-dose settings. Individual module evaluations demonstrated the reliability of the agentic components, while the complete framework achieved higher PSNR and SSIM than UNet, GAN, and DDPM baselines at both dose levels. These preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of using a closed-loop multi-agent framework to adapt PET denoising strategies to different image conditions.

</details>


### [187] [From crown candidates to neighborhood screening: integrating optical GeoAI and spatial modeling for urban-canopy assessment in Davis, California](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13856)
*Mohammadreza Narimani,Shreyan Mitra,Parastoo Farajpoor*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光学GeoAI工作流来测绘城市树冠的方法，并应用于加州戴维斯市。该方法结合DeepForest树冠检测、NDVI阈值、非极大值抑制和SAM分割，绘制了2.43 km²的树冠面积（占城市面积9.37%），通过与LiDAR辅助产品对比验证了准确性，并展示了树冠与地表温度的负相关关系及道路邻近性特征，最终生成可复现、可更新的规划筛选图层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 及时的城市树冠信息对于将遥感数据与热环境、交通流动和社区规划联系起来至关重要。现有结构性的树冠产品（如LiDAR辅助产品）获取成本高、更新周期长，需要一种基于光学影像的、可重复更新且成本更低的工作流来补充现有产品，为城市规划和热环境分析提供快速的筛选层。

Method: 采用光学GeoAI工作流：使用2022年NAIP影像（0.6 m RGB+NIR），分三步处理——(1) DeepForest生成树冠候选框；(2) NDVI阈值、非极大值抑制筛选候选框；(3) 以候选框为提示输入Segment Anything Model (ViT-B)生成树冠锚定的树冠表面。分析基于25.92 km²的Census TIGER市政边界和100 m网格，并与2022年USDA/CAL FIRE LiDAR辅助树冠产品对比验证，同时结合Landsat地表温度和道路数据进行空间分析（Spearman相关、偏相关、空间滞后模型），最终生成注意力表面图。

Result: 工作流保留了11,741个候选树冠，绘制了2.43 km²树冠覆盖（占城市9.37%）。在相同范围内，87.8%的树冠像素和97.4%的候选中心与LiDAR辅助产品一致；光学表面代表参考树冠面积的34.2%（IoU 0.288；Dice 0.448）。约49%的候选位于道路15 m内。树冠与Landsat地表温度呈负相关（Spearman rho = -0.293；控制建筑概率后偏rho = -0.370），空间滞后模型确认了明显的邻域结构。生成的两个注意力表面结合了树冠需求与热和情境指标。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可复现、可更新的筛选图层，能够补充结构性树冠产品和市政清单，同时保留假设、数据来源和空间诊断信息以供规划解读。虽然光学表面在面积上仅覆盖参考产品的34.2%，但其定位精度较高（候选中心一致性97.4%），可作为快速筛查和补充工具，为城市规划、热环境评估和绿色基础设施管理提供支持。

Abstract: Timely urban-canopy information is essential for linking remote sensing with heat, mobility, and neighborhood planning. We developed an optical GeoAI workflow for Davis, California, using 2022 National Agriculture Imagery Program imagery (0.6 m RGB+NIR). DeepForest generated crown candidates; an NDVI threshold, non-maximum suppression, and box-prompted Segment Anything Model (ViT-B) produced a crown-anchored canopy surface. Analyses used the 25.92 km2 Census TIGER municipal boundary and a 100 m grid. The workflow retained 11,741 candidate crowns and mapped 2.43 km2 of canopy (9.37% of the city). On the identical extent, 87.8% of mapped canopy pixels and 97.4% of candidate centers agreed with the 2022 USDA/CAL FIRE LiDAR-assisted canopy product; the optical surface represented 34.2% of the reference canopy area (IoU 0.288; Dice 0.448). Approximately 49% of candidates occurred within 15 m of a road. Canopy was inversely associated with Landsat land-surface temperature (Spearman rho = -0.293; partial rho = -0.370 controlling for built probability), and spatial-lag modeling confirmed clear neighborhood structure. Two transparent attention surfaces combined canopy need with thermal and contextual indicators. The framework provides a reproducible, updateable screening layer that complements structural canopy products and municipal inventories while retaining assumptions, data provenance, and spatial diagnostics for planning interpretation.

</details>


### [188] [Practical Lossless Volumetric Medical Image Compression via Tri-plane Context Tree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13897)
*Yuanchao Bai,Yifan Zhao,Kai Wang,Yuanbo Du,Jie Cheng,Teng Fang,Xianming Liu,Wen Gao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于三平面上下文树（TCT）的无损体积医学图像压缩方法，无需DNN或外部训练数据，通过分解3D上下文建模为三个正交平面的2D建模，结合自适应二叉树结构和MDL优化进行逐样本学习，达到与DNN方法相当的压缩性能同时保持低计算成本和快速编码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无损压缩方法因依赖刚性手工模型而效率受限，而DNN-based压缩方法虽然有效但计算资源消耗大，难以在资源受限的临床和研究环境中部署。需要一种既高效又轻量、无需外部训练数据的无损体积医学图像压缩方案。

Method: 提出三平面上下文树（TCT）方法：1）构建紧凑的三平面上下文表示，将复杂3D上下文建模分解为三个正交平面上的高效2D建模，利用片内和片间冗余；2）将该表示与上下文树框架集成，构建输入自适应的TCT模型，采用自适应二叉树结构；3）每个节点动态选择三平面预测器和上下文特征提取器，实现数据自适应的上下文建模；4）通过采样子集、以MDL准则优化进行迭代构建和剪枝来学习模型（无需离线训练）；5）每个像素检索对应上下文，按上下文指定的预测器计算残差，基于关联直方图进行熵编码。

Result: 在多个数据集上，TCT方法实现了与近期DNN-based方法相当的压缩性能，同时保持低计算成本和快速编码速度，适用于实际部署。

Conclusion: TCT方法在无DNN、无外部训练数据的条件下达到了与DNN方法可比的压缩性能，平衡了压缩效率与计算开销，为资源受限环境下的体积医学图像无损压缩提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Lossless compression of volumetric medical images is of paramount importance for clinical and research applications where data fidelity is essential. Traditional compression methods are often limited in efficiency due to rigid, handcrafted models. Conversely, deep neural network (DNN)-based compression methods, while effective, demand substantial computational resources, hindering deployment in resource-constrained settings. To address these challenges, we propose a novel tri-plane context tree (TCT)-based method for lossless volumetric medical image compression that delivers high performance without relying on DNNs or external training data. To exploit intra-slice and inter-slice redundancies, we introduce a compact tri-plane context representation that decomposes complex 3D context modeling into efficient 2D modeling on three orthogonal planes. By integrating this representation with a context tree framework, we develop an input-specific TCT model employing an adaptive binary tree structure. At each tree node, the model dynamically selects from a suite of tri-plane based predictors and contextual feature extractors, enabling data-adaptive context modeling tailored to local structural characteristics. Instead of offline training, we sample a subset of the input volume to learn the TCT model by optimizing the minimum description length (MDL) through iterative construction and pruning. With the learned TCT model, each pixel retrieves its corresponding context, computes the prediction residual using the predictor dictated by the context, and performs entropy encoding based on the associated histograms. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves compression performance on par with recent DNN-based methods on multiple datasets, while maintaining low computational cost and fast coding speeds, making it highly applicable in practice.

</details>


### [189] [A Subjective Study on a New Sharpness Informed Class of Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13989)
*Uditangshu Aurangabadkar,Vibhoothi Vibhoothi,Darren Ramsook,Anil Kokaram*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文针对DNN去模糊架构中的感知损失函数缺乏对锐度显式优化的不足，通过四协议主观研究，引入均匀锐度递增数据集及DMOS评分，提出锐度感知（SI）图像质量评估（IQA）指标（如SI-PSNR），证明锐度感知复合损失在67%的二值化比较中优于非锐度显式损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN去模糊领域的感知损失函数虽能提升图像整体质量，但鲜有研究显式针对恢复图像的锐度进行优化，缺乏对锐度水平偏好及过度锐化惩罚的系统性研究。

Method: 采用四协议方法进行主观研究，探索偏好锐度水平及对图像质量的影响；构建均匀锐度递增的新数据集并附带DMOS评分；提出一类新的锐度感知（SI）图像质量评估指标，其中SI-PSNR被设计用于恰当惩罚过度锐化。

Result: SI-PSNR在IQA基准数据集上的相关性统计优于所有其他PSNR变体；使用锐度感知复合损失的模型在67%的二值化比较中优于未显式针对锐度的损失。

Conclusion: 锐度感知的损失函数和评估指标能显著提升去模糊图像的主观偏好度，SI-PSNR可作为更优的锐度相关IQA指标。

Abstract: Perceptual loss functions in Deep Neural Network (DNN) deblurring architectures improve the overall quality of restored images. However, few focus on explicitly targeting sharpness in the restorations. We conduct a subjective study of models trained with and without losses which explicitly target sharpness using a four-protocol approach, exploring preferred sharpness levels and effects on image quality. We introduce a novel dataset of images with uniform sharpness increments along with Difference Mean Opinion Scores (DMOS). Additionally, we propose a novel class of Sharpness Informed (SI) Image Quality Assessment (IQA) metrics which properly penalize over-sharpening. Our new SI-PSNR metric outperforms all other PSNR variants in terms of correlation statistics on IQA benchmarking datasets. We show that, on average, images restored using a sharpness-aware composite loss are preferred in 67% of binarized comparisons, as opposed to losses that do not explicitly target sharpness.

</details>


### [190] [Personalized Digital Semantic Communication for Image Transmission with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14260)
*Nan Li,Li Zhou,Haijun Wang,Jun Xiong,Haitao Zhao,Jibo Wei*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种个性化数字语义通信（PDSC）框架，通过集成基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义编码器和基于潜在扩散模型（LDM）的语义解码器，实现接收者感知的个性化无线图像语义传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数语义通信（SC）方案是用户无关的（user-agnostic），忽略了接收者相关的语义信息，无法根据接收者的个性化偏好进行图像传输。

Method: 1) 语义编码器从源图像和接收者历史交互中提取源感知的个性化语义令牌，经向量量化得到离散语义索引，并编码为紧凑的固定长度比特流以兼容数字传输；2) 语义解码器基于恢复的语义令牌重建个性化图像；3) 构建容量约束下的个性化语义率失真问题，引入联合刻画源-语义保真度和用户偏好对齐的语义失真度量。

Result: 在带宽受限的无线传输条件下，PDSC在源-语义一致性和个性化方面优于最先进的SC基线方法（如CDDM和MoS）。

Conclusion: 通过将VLM和LDM引入语义通信框架并提出新的语义失真度量，PDSC实现了带宽高效且用户个性化的无线图像传输，为个性化语义通信提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SC) enables bandwidth-efficient wireless image transmission, but most existing SC schemes are user-agnostic and ignore receiver-dependent semantics. To address this issue, we propose a personalized digital semantic communication (PDSC) framework that integrates a vision-language model (VLM)-based semantic encoder with a latent diffusion model (LDM)-based semantic decoder. Specifically, the semantic encoder extracts source-aware personalized semantic tokens from both the source image and the receiver's historical interactions. These tokens are vector-quantized into discrete semantic indices and further encoded into a compact fixed-length bitstream, enabling compatibility with digital transmission. At the receiver, the semantic decoder reconstructs a personalized image conditioned on the recovered semantic tokens. Furthermore, we formulate a capacity-constrained personalized semantic rate-distortion problem and introduce a semantic distortion metric that jointly characterizes source-semantic fidelity and user-preference alignment. Experiments show that PDSC achieves superior source-semantic consistency and personalization over state-of-the-art SC baselines, including CDDM and MoS, under bandwidth-limited wireless transmission.

</details>


### [191] [UMPIRE-Net: Unrolled Magnitude-Phase Regularization Network for Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14422)
*Mahdi Saberi,Toygan Kiliç,Mehmet Akçakaya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: UMPIRE-Net提出一种基于物理驱动的深度学习MRI重建方法，将图像重建分解为幅度和相位两个独立学习正则化器，并引入新的数据保真项来强制执行测量一致性，在部分傅里叶(PF)加速MRI重建中优于传统复值基线，获得更清晰的图像和更少的伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理驱动深度学习(PD-DL)方法通常直接重建复值图像，将幅度和相位隐式耦合在单一学习表示中。这种耦合正则化在相位建模重要的场景（如部分傅里叶PF成像）中可能是次优的，因为PF成像中恢复被省略的非对称k空间测量依赖于底层图像相位。因此需要显式分离幅度和相位的建模方式，以降低对外部估计或预定义相位信息的依赖。

Method: 提出UMPIRE-Net（Unrolled Magnitude-Phase In REgularization Network），一种PD-DL方法，其核心包括：(1) 为幅度和相位分量分别设计独立的可学习正则化器；(2) 提出一种新的数据保真度公式，以强制实现测量一致性。该方法在算法展开(unrolling)框架内将MRI前向模型与分离的幅度-相位学习正则化相结合。

Result: 在多种数据集和加速因子下，针对带部分傅里叶(PF)的加速MRI进行评估，UMPIRE-Net较传统复值PD-DL基线方法显著提升了重建质量，产生更锐利的图像并减少伪影。

Conclusion: 显式分离幅度和相位作为独立正则化分量是有效的MRI重建策略，特别适用于相位信息起关键作用的部分傅里叶成像场景，可提升重建质量和鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Abstract: MRI reconstruction from undersampled k-space measurements is an ill-posed inverse problem. Physics-driven deep learning (PD-DL) methods have shown strong performance for this task by combining the MRI forward model with learned image regularization within algorithm-unrolling frameworks. However, most existing PD-DL methods reconstruct complex-valued images directly, thereby implicitly coupling magnitude and phase within a single learned representation. This coupled regularization may be suboptimal in reconstruction settings where accurate phase modeling plays an important role, such as partial Fourier (PF) imaging, where recovery of the omitted asymmetric k-space measurements depends on the underlying image phase. In such scenarios, explicit modeling of magnitude and phase as separate components may reduce the reliance on externally estimated or predefined phase information. To this end, we propose UMPIRE-Net (Unrolled Magnitude-Phase In REgularization Network), a PD-DL method that introduces separate learned regularizers for magnitude and phase components, together with a novel data-fidelity formulation that enforces measurements consistency. We evaluate UMPIRE-Net for accelerated MRI with PF across different datasets and acceleration factors. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed method improves reconstruction quality compared with a conventional complex-valued PD-DL baseline, yielding sharper images and reduced artifacts. Code available at: https://github.com/MahdiSaberii/UMPIRE-Net

</details>
